{"body":"## Bug\n\nWhen updating the main agent identity or soul via the Settings UI, the files are written to `data_dir/IDENTITY.md` and `data_dir/SOUL.md` (root) instead of `data_dir/agents/main/IDENTITY.md` and `data_dir/agents/main/SOUL.md`.\n\nHowever, both `load_identity_for_agent(\"main\")` and `load_soul_for_agent(\"main\")` check `agents/main/` **first** and only fall back to root. So if the agent-level files already exist, the settings updates are silently ignored at runtime.\n\n## Root Cause\n\n### IDENTITY.md\n\nThe call chain for the main agent identity update:\n\n1. **Settings UI** (`identity-utils.js`) calls `updateIdentity(fields, { agentId: \"main\" })`\n2. This sends RPC `agents.identity.update` with `agent_id: \"main\"`\n3. If that fails (method not found fallback), it sends `agent.identity.update` without agent_id\n4. **`agent.identity.update`** handler (`services.rs:322-330`): for main agent, delegates to `onboarding.identity_update()`\n5. **`onboarding.identity_update()`** (`service.rs:271`): calls `moltis_config::save_identity(&identity)` — writes to **root** `data_dir/IDENTITY.md`\n\nBut **`load_identity_for_agent(\"main\")`** (`loader.rs:374-380`) reads from `agents/main/IDENTITY.md` first and only falls back to root:\n\n```rust\npub fn load_identity_for_agent(agent_id: &str) -> Option<AgentIdentity> {\n    if agent_id == \"main\" {\n        let main_path = agent_workspace_dir(\"main\").join(\"IDENTITY.md\");\n        if let Some(identity) = load_identity_from_path(&main_path) {\n            return Some(identity);\n        }\n        return load_identity();  // fallback to root\n    }\n    // ...\n}\n```\n\n### SOUL.md (same pattern)\n\nThe save path for soul updates:\n\n1. **Settings UI** calls `agent.identity.update_soul`\n2. **`identity_update_soul()`** (`service.rs:295`): calls `moltis_config::save_soul()` — writes to **root** `data_dir/SOUL.md`\n\nBut **`load_soul_for_agent(\"main\")`** (`loader.rs:515-523`) reads from `agents/main/SOUL.md` first:\n\n```rust\npub fn load_soul_for_agent(agent_id: &str) -> Option<String> {\n    if agent_id == \"main\" {\n        let main_path = agent_workspace_dir(\"main\").join(\"SOUL.md\");\n        if let Some(soul) = load_workspace_markdown(main_path) {\n            return Some(soul);\n        }\n        return load_soul();  // fallback to root\n    }\n    // ...\n}\n```\n\nMeanwhile, `save_soul()` writes to `soul_path()` which is `data_dir()/SOUL.md` (root).\n\n## Consequence\n\n- Both root `IDENTITY.md` and `SOUL.md` should not be primary write targets for the main agent\n- If `agents/main/` versions exist, any changes through onboarding or the settings page are invisible\n- The UI string \"Saved to IDENTITY.md in your workspace root\" is misleading\n\n## Suggested Fix\n\n### IDENTITY.md\nIn `crates/onboarding/src/service.rs`, both at line 110 and line 271, replace:\n\n```rust\nmoltis_config::save_identity(&ws.identity)\n```\n\nwith:\n\n```rust\nmoltis_config::save_identity_for_agent(\"main\", &ws.identity)\n```\n\n### SOUL.md\nIn `crates/onboarding/src/service.rs` line 295, the `identity_update_soul()` method should call a new `save_soul_for_agent(\"main\", soul)` function (analogous to `save_identity_for_agent`) that writes to `agents/main/SOUL.md` instead of root.\n\nAlternatively, `save_soul()` could be updated to always write to the agent workspace when the agent_id is known.\n\nThe root files can remain as legacy fallbacks for installations that do not yet have `agents/main/` versions.\n\n## Steps to Reproduce\n\n1. Ensure `data_dir/agents/main/IDENTITY.md` and `data_dir/agents/main/SOUL.md` exist\n2. Edit agent name or soul text via Settings UI\n3. Observe root-level files are updated, but agent-level files are unchanged\n4. Runtime continues using the old agent-level files\n","comments":[],"labels":[],"title":"Settings UI writes IDENTITY.md and SOUL.md to root instead of agents/main/ workspace"}

> AGENT

Now I have a clear picture. Here's the fix:

## Issue Summary

The Settings UI saves `IDENTITY.md` and `SOUL.md` to root (`~/.moltis/`) but the loader reads from `agents/main/` first. If agent-level files exist, UI changes are silently ignored.

## Fix (3 changes in `crates/onboarding/src/service.rs`)

### 1. Line 110 — onboarding wizard completion
```rust
// Before:
moltis_config::save_identity(&ws.identity).context("failed to save IDENTITY.md")?;

// After:
moltis_config::save_identity_for_agent("main", &ws.identity).context("failed to save IDENTITY.md")?;
```

### 2. Line 271 — `identity_update` (Settings UI)
```rust
// Before:
moltis_config::save_identity(&identity).context("failed to save identity")?;

// After:
moltis_config::save_identity_for_agent("main", &identity).context("failed to save identity")?;
```

### 3. Line 297 — `identity_update_soul` (Settings UI)

`save_soul_for_agent` doesn't exist yet, so it needs to be added to `crates/config/src/loader.rs`, mirroring the existing `save_identity_for_agent` pattern:

```rust
pub fn save_soul_for_agent(agent_id: &str, soul: Option<&str>) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {
    let dir = agent_workspace_dir(agent_id);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir)?;
    let path = dir.join("SOUL.md");
    match soul.map(str::trim) {
        Some(content) if !content.is_empty() => {
            std::fs::write(&path, content)?;
        }
        _ => {
            std::fs::write(&path, "")?;
        }
    }
    Ok(path)
}
```

Then in `service.rs:297`:
```rust
// Before:
moltis_config::save_soul(soul.as_deref()).context("failed to save soul")?;

// After:
moltis_config::save_soul_for_agent("main", soul.as_deref()).context("failed to save soul")?;
```

And export `save_soul_for_agent` from `crates/config/src/lib.rs` alongside the existing `save_identity_for_agent`.

### Why keep root `save_identity`/`save_soul`?

They serve as legacy fallback writers. The loaders already fall back to root when `agents/main/` files don't exist, so older installations without the `agents/main/` directory continue to work. No changes needed to the load paths.

Want me to implement this?

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/soul-location/crates/config/src/loader.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Persist SOUL.md in the workspace root (`data_dir`).\n///\n/// - `Some(non-empty)` writes `SOUL.md` with the given content\n/// - `None` or empty writes an empty `SOUL.md` so that `load_soul()`\n///   returns `None` without re-seeding the default\npub fn save_soul(soul: Option<&str>) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {\n    let path = soul_path();\n    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {\n        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;\n    }\n    match soul.map(str::trim) {\n        Some(content) if !content.is_empty() => {\n            std::fs::write(&path, content)?;\n        },\n        _ => {\n            // Write an empty file rather than deleting so `load_soul()`\n            // distinguishes \"user cleared soul\" from \"file never existed\".\n            std::fs::write(&path, \"\")?;\n        },\n    }\n    Ok(path)\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Persist SOUL.md in the workspace root (`data_dir`).\n///\n/// - `Some(non-empty)` writes `SOUL.md` with the given content\n/// - `None` or empty writes an empty `SOUL.md` so that `load_soul()`\n///   returns `None` without re-seeding the default\npub fn save_soul(soul: Option<&str>) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {\n    let path = soul_path();\n    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {\n        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;\n    }\n    match soul.map(str::trim) {\n        Some(content) if !content.is_empty() => {\n            std::fs::write(&path, content)?;\n        },\n        _ => {\n            // Write an empty file rather than deleting so `load_soul()`\n            // distinguishes \"user cleared soul\" from \"file never existed\".\n            std::fs::write(&path, \"\")?;\n        },\n    }\n    Ok(path)\n}\n\n/// Persist SOUL.md into an agent's workspace directory.\n///\n/// For the main agent this writes to `agents/main/SOUL.md` so that\n/// `load_soul_for_agent(\"main\")` picks it up on the primary read path.\npub fn save_soul_for_agent(agent_id: &str, soul: Option<&str>) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {\n    let dir = agent_workspace_dir(agent_id);\n    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir)?;\n    let path = dir.join(\"SOUL.md\");\n    match soul.map(str::trim) {\n        Some(content) if !content.is_empty() => {\n            std::fs::write(&path, content)?;\n        },\n        _ => {\n            std::fs::write(&path, \"\")?;\n        },\n    }\n    Ok(path)\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

Line 285 uses `load_soul()` (root-only) to return the soul in the response after an identity update. This should use `load_soul_for_agent("main")` to be consistent — otherwise the response would show stale data if an agent-level SOUL.md already existed.

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/soul-location/crates/config/src/loader.rs",
  "old_string": "    // ── share_dir tests ─────────────────────────────────────────────────",
  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn save_soul_for_agent_writes_to_agent_dir() {\n        let _guard = DATA_DIR_TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect(\"tempdir\");\n        set_data_dir(dir.path().to_path_buf());\n\n        let custom = \"Agent soul content.\";\n        save_soul_for_agent(\"main\", Some(custom)).expect(\"save_soul_for_agent\");\n\n        let agent_soul = dir.path().join(\"agents/main/SOUL.md\");\n        assert!(agent_soul.exists(), \"SOUL.md should exist in agents/main/\");\n        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&agent_soul).unwrap(), custom);\n\n        // load_soul_for_agent must find the agent-level file.\n        let loaded = load_soul_for_agent(\"main\");\n        assert_eq!(loaded.as_deref(), Some(custom));\n\n        clear_data_dir();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn save_soul_for_agent_none_clears() {\n        let _guard = DATA_DIR_TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect(\"tempdir\");\n        set_data_dir(dir.path().to_path_buf());\n\n        save_soul_for_agent(\"main\", Some(\"initial\")).expect(\"save\");\n        save_soul_for_agent(\"main\", None).expect(\"clear\");\n\n        let agent_soul = dir.path().join(\"agents/main/SOUL.md\");\n        assert!(agent_soul.exists(), \"file should remain after clearing\");\n        assert!(\n            std::fs::read_to_string(&agent_soul).unwrap().is_empty(),\n            \"file should be empty after clearing\"\n        );\n\n        clear_data_dir();\n    }\n\n    // ── share_dir tests ─────────────────────────────────────────────────"
}

     Ok(path)
 }
 
+/// Persist SOUL.md into an agent's workspace directory.
+///
+/// For the main agent this writes to `agents/main/SOUL.md` so that
+/// `load_soul_for_agent("main")` picks it up on the primary read path.
+pub fn save_soul_for_agent(agent_id: &str, soul: Option<&str>) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {
+    let dir = agent_workspace_dir(agent_id);
+    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir)?;
+    let path = dir.join("SOUL.md");
+    match soul.map(str::trim) {
+        Some(content) if !content.is_empty() => {
+            std::fs::write(&path, content)?;
+        },
+        _ => {
+            std::fs::write(&path, "")?;
+        },
+    }
+    Ok(path)
+}
+
 /// Persist identity values to `IDENTITY.md` using YAML frontmatter.
 pub fn save_identity(identity: &AgentIdentity) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {
     let path = identity_path();
@@ -643,7 +662,7 @@ pub fn save_identity(identity: &AgentIdentity) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {
     Ok(path)
 }
 
-/// Persist identity values for a non-main agent into its workspace.
+/// Persist identity values for an agent into its workspace directory.
 pub fn save_identity_for_agent(agent_id: &str, identity: &AgentIdentity) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {
     let dir = agent_workspace_dir(agent_id);
     std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir)?;
@@ -1860,6 +1879,45 @@ name = "Rex"
         clear_data_dir();
     }
 
+    #[test]
+    fn save_soul_for_agent_writes_to_agent_dir() {
+        let _guard = DATA_DIR_TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("tempdir");
+        set_data_dir(dir.path().to_path_buf());
+
+        let custom = "Agent soul content.";
+        save_soul_for_agent("main", Some(custom)).expect("save_soul_for_agent");
+
+        let agent_soul = dir.path().join("agents/main/SOUL.md");
+        assert!(agent_soul.exists(), "SOUL.md should exist in agents/main/");
+        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&agent_soul).unwrap(), custom);
+
+        // load_soul_for_agent must find the agent-level file.
+        let loaded = load_soul_for_agent("main");
+        assert_eq!(loaded.as_deref(), Some(custom));
+
+        clear_data_dir();
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn save_soul_for_agent_none_clears() {
+        let _guard = DATA_DIR_TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
+        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("tempdir");
+        set_data_dir(dir.path().to_path_buf());
+
+        save_soul_for_agent("main", Some("initial")).expect("save");
+        save_soul_for_agent("main", None).expect("clear");
+
+        let agent_soul = dir.path().join("agents/main/SOUL.md");
+        assert!(agent_soul.exists(), "file should remain after clearing");
+        assert!(
+            std::fs::read_to_string(&agent_soul).unwrap().is_empty(),
+            "file should be empty after clearing"
+        );
+
+        clear_data_dir();
+    }
+
     // ── share_dir tests ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 
     #[test]

The pinentry curses program can't get input from within this terminal. The issue is that `pinentry-curses` needs an interactive TTY. You'll likely need to run the commit manually in your own terminal so pinentry can prompt you for the YubiKey touch:

```bash
git add crates/config/src/lib.rs crates/config/src/loader.rs crates/onboarding/src/service.rs
git commit -m 'fix(config): write IDENTITY.md and SOUL.md to agents/main/ instead of root

Settings UI and onboarding wrote these files to `data_dir/` root, but the
loaders check `data_dir/agents/main/` first.  When agent-level files
already existed, updates via the UI were silently ignored at runtime.

- Add `save_soul_for_agent()` mirroring existing `save_identity_for_agent()`
- Switch onboarding wizard and settings handlers to save to `agents/main/`
- Fix `identity_update` response to read soul from `load_soul_for_agent`
- Add tests for the new `save_soul_for_agent` function

Closes #376'
```

Once committed, let me know and I'll push and create the PR.

> AGENT

The function currently ignores `config` entirely — it only generates args for `moltis node run` without passing gateway URL, token, etc. The test expects those config values to appear in the plist. This is clearly a pre-existing incomplete implementation where `config` was meant to be used to add environment variables or extra arguments. The test was written for the intended behavior but the implementation wasn't completed.

The simplest correct fix: include config values as environment variables in the plist, which is how launchd services typically receive configuration.

1.0//EN\" \"http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd\">\n<plist version=\"1.0\">\n<dict>\n  <key>Label</key>\n  <string>{LAUNCHD_LABEL}</string>\n  <key>ProgramArguments</key>\n > AGENT

The test creates a repo with `git init -b main` and commits, then `resolve_base_branch` runs `git rev-parse --verify main` which should work. This is likely a git signing issue in the test environment — the `git commit --allow-empty` might be failing silently due to GPG signing, leaving no commits and thus no `main` ref.

> AGENT

Confirmed — the global `commit.gpgsign = true` config is making `git commit` in the test repo fail because pinentry can't prompt. This is a local environment issue, not a code bug. The test passes in CI and would pass on your machine with a working pinentry.

These 2 worktree test failures are environment-specific (GPG signing can't prompt in this terminal). They're not code issues and will pass in CI. The PR's own changes are all passing. Want me to re-run the validate script with `--no-fail-fast` to confirm all other tests pass, or are you comfortable merging based on CI results?

     1→// ── Shared identity RPC wrappers and validation ───────────────
     2→//
     3→// Used by page-settings.js and onboarding-view.js.
     4→
     5→import { sendRpc } from "./helpers.js";
     6→
     7→/**
     8→ * Validate identity fields before submission.
     9→ * @param {string} name - agent name
    10→ * @param {string} userName - user's name
    11→ * @returns {{ valid: true } | { valid: false, error: string }}
    12→ */
    13→export function validateIdentityFields(name, userName) {
    14→	if (!(name.trim() || userName.trim())) {
    15→		return { valid: false, error: "Agent name and your name are required." };
    16→	}
    17→	if (!name.trim()) {
    18→		return { valid: false, error: "Agent name is required." };
    19→	}
    20→	if (!userName.trim()) {
    21→		return { valid: false, error: "Your name is required." };
    22→	}
    23→	return { valid: true };
    24→}
    25→
    26→function isMissingMethodError(res) {
    27→	var message = res?.error?.message;
    28→	if (typeof message !== "string") return false;
    29→	var lower = message.toLowerCase();
    30→	return lower.includes("method") && (lower.includes("not found") || lower.includes("unknown"));
    31→}
    32→
    33→/**
    34→ * Update agent identity fields.
    35→ * @param {object} fields - any subset of { name, emoji, theme, soul, user_name, user_timezone }
    36→ * @param {{ agentId?: string }} [options] - optional explicit agent target
    37→ * @returns {Promise} RPC response
    38→ */
    39→export function updateIdentity(fields, options = {}) {
    40→	var agentId = options.agentId;
    41→	if (!agentId) {
    42→		return sendRpc("agent.identity.update", fields);
    43→	}
    44→	var params = { ...fields, agent_id: agentId };
    45→	return sendRpc("agents.identity.update", params).then((res) => {
    46→		if (res?.ok || !isMissingMethodError(res)) return res;
    47→		return sendRpc("agent.identity.update", fields);
    48→	});
    49→}
    50→

```
     1→// ── Server-injected data (gon pattern) ────────────────────
     2→//
     3→// The server injects `window.__MOLTIS__ = { ... }` into every
     4→// page <head> before any module script runs.  This module
     5→// provides typed access, runtime updates, and a refresh
     6→// mechanism that re-fetches the data from `/api/gon`.
     7→//
     8→// Register listeners with `onChange(key, fn)` to react when
     9→// a key is updated (via `set()` or `refresh()`).
    10→
    11→var gon = window.__MOLTIS__ || {};
    12→var listeners = {};
    13→
    14→export function get(key) {
    15→	return gon[key] ?? null;
    16→}
    17→
    18→export function set(key, value) {
    19→	gon[key] = value;
    20→	notify(key, value);
    21→}
    22→
    23→export function onChange(key, fn) {
    24→	if (!listeners[key]) listeners[key] = [];
    25→	listeners[key].push(fn);
    26→}
    27→
    28→export function refresh() {
    29→	return fetch(`/api/gon?_=${Date.now()}`, {
    30→		cache: "no-store",
    31→		headers: {
    32→			"Cache-Control": "no-cache",
    33→			Pragma: "no-cache",
    34→		},
    35→	})
    36→		.then((r) => (r.ok ? r.json() : null))
    37→		.then((data) => {
    38→			if (!data) return;
    39→			for (var key of Object.keys(data)) {
    40→				gon[key] = data[key];
    41→				notify(key, data[key]);
    42→			}
    43→		});
    44→}
    45→
    46→function notify(key, value) {
    47→	for (var fn of listeners[key] || []) fn(value);
    48→}
    49→
```

Then `window.__MOLTIS__.identity.name` should be updated. The test polls it and expects "AutoBotNameA" but gets "e2e-bot".

> AGENT

So `flashSaved()` only runs on `res?.ok` (line 367). "Saved" IS appearing, which means the RPC succeeded. But then `gon.set("identity", res.payload)` sets the identity, and then `refreshGon()` re-fetches `/api/gon` which would overwrite it.

The race: `refreshGon()` calls `gon.refresh()` which fetches `/api/gon`. The `/api/gon` handler calls `identity_get()` which reads from `moltis.toml`. But wait — `identity_get()` uses `resolve_identity_from_config` which merges config with `load_identity()` / `load_identity_for_agent()`.

Let me check `resolve_identity_from_config` and `resolve_identity`.

> AGENT

There it is! Line 399: `resolve_identity_from_config` calls `load_identity()` (root `IDENTITY.md`), NOT `load_identity_for_agent("main")`. And line 417: `id.soul = load_soul()` (root), not `load_soul_for_agent("main")`.

So the flow is:
1. Identity update saves to `agents/main/IDENTITY.md` (my change)
2. `gon.set("identity", res.payload)` sets the correct name in JS
3. `refreshGon()` re-fetches `/api/gon`  
4. `/api/gon` → `identity_get()` → `resolve_identity_from_config()` → `load_identity()` reads from **root** `IDENTITY.md` which still has the old name
5. This overwrites the JS gon identity back to the old name

The fix: `resolve_identity_from_config` should read from `agents/main/` when resolving the main agent's identity. Or more directly — the `identity_get` method should use `load_identity_for_agent("main")`.

> TOOL

id: toolu_01DnWoN8xeb7JMN11BbNCGog

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/soul-location/crates/config/src/loader.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Like [`resolve_identity`] but accepts a pre-loaded config.\npub fn resolve_identity_from_config(config: &MoltisConfig) -> ResolvedIdentity {\n    let mut id = ResolvedIdentity::from_config(config);\n\n    if let Some(file_identity) = load_identity() {\n        if let Some(name) = file_identity.name {\n            id.name = name;\n        }\n        if let Some(emoji) = file_identity.emoji {\n            id.emoji = Some(emoji);\n        }\n        if let Some(theme) = file_identity.theme {\n            id.theme = Some(theme);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if let Some(file_user) = load_user()\n        && let Some(name) = file_user.name\n    {\n        id.user_name = Some(name);\n    }\n\n    id.soul = load_soul();\n    id\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Like [`resolve_identity`] but accepts a pre-loaded config.\npub fn resolve_identity_from_config(config: &MoltisConfig) -> ResolvedIdentity {\n    let mut id = ResolvedIdentity::from_config(config);\n\n    // Read from `agents/main/IDENTITY.md` first (primary), falling back to\n    // root `IDENTITY.md` (legacy).  This mirrors the read path in\n    // `load_identity_for_agent(\"main\")`.\n    if let Some(file_identity) = load_identity_for_agent(\"main\") {\n        if let Some(name) = file_identity.name {\n            id.name = name;\n        }\n        if let Some(emoji) = file_identity.emoji {\n            id.emoji = Some(emoji);\n        }\n        if let Some(theme) = file_identity.theme {\n            id.theme = Some(theme);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if let Some(file_user) = load_user()\n        && let Some(name) = file_user.name\n    {\n        id.user_name = Some(name);\n    }\n\n    id.soul = load_soul_for_agent(\"main\");\n    id\n}"
}

`agents/main/IDENTITY.md`, but it still merges from `load_identity()` (root `IDENTITY.md`) before applying partial updates. On upgraded installs that already have a root identity file, partial saves (like name/emoji autosaves) can re-import stale root fields and write them back, reverting newer values in config and `agents/main/IDENTITY.md`. Use `load_identity_for_agent(\"main\")` (or stop merging root identity here) so partial updates do not roll back other fields.\n\nUseful? -607,6 +607,25 @@ pub fn save_soul(soul: Option<&str>) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {\n     Ok(path)\n }\n \n+/// Persist SOUL.md into an agent's workspace directory.\n+///\n+/// For the main agent this writes to `agents/main/SOUL.md` so that\n+/// `load_soul_for_agent(\"main\")` picks it up on the primary read path.\n+pub fn save_soul_for_agent(agent_id: &str, soul: Option<&str>) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {\n+    let dir = agent_workspace_dir(agent_id);\n+    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir)?;\n+    let path = dir.join(\"SOUL.md\");\n+    match soul.map(str::trim) {\n+        Some(content) if !content.is_empty() => {\n+            std::fs::write(&path, content)?;\n+        },\n+        _ => {\n+            std::fs::write(&path, \"\")?;\n+        },","path":"crates/config/src/loader.rs","commit_id":"a2282766ea5fe413add7046aa2a0bf6deb7a076a","original_commit_id":"baf06f12ad076994b5db7bfa728caa39f244e8f7","user":{"login":"chatgpt-codex-connector[bot]","id":199175422,"node_id":"BOT_kgDOC98s_g","avatar_url":"https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/in/1144995?v=4","gravatar_id":"","url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D","html_url":"https://github.com/apps/chatgpt-codex-connector","followers_url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D/followers","following_url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D/following{/other_user}","gists_url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D/gists{/gist_id}","starred_url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D/starred{/owner}{/repo}","subscriptions_url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D/subscriptions","organizations_url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D/orgs","repos_url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D/repos","events_url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D/events{/privacy}","received_events_url":"https://api.github.com/users/chatgpt-codex-connector%5Bbot%5D/received_events","type":"Bot","user_view_type":"public","site_admin":false},"body":"**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Preserve explicit soul clears for main agent**\n\nWhen `save_soul_for_agent(\"main\", None)` runs, it writes an empty `agents/main/SOUL.md`. `load_soul_for_agent(\"main\")` treats that empty file as `None` and then falls back to root `load_soul()`, so a clear operation can immediately re-surface old/default soul text instead of remaining cleared. Since `identity_update_soul` now uses this function for main, clearing soul no longer persists reliably.\n\nUseful? The fix correctly adds `save_soul_for_agent` and redirects most call sites to the agent-scoped path.\n\n**Key changes:**\n- New `save_soul_for_agent(agent_id, soul)` function in `loader.rs`, with tests covering both write and clear semantics — well-structured and mirrors the existing `save_identity_for_agent` pattern.\n- `wizard_next` and the identity-saving path in `identity_update` now correctly call `save_identity_for_agent(\"main\", ...)`.\n- `identity_update_soul` now correctly calls `save_soul_for_agent(\"main\", ...)`.\n- `identity_update` response now reads soul via `load_soul_for_agent(\"main\")`.\n\n**Issue found:**\n- The `identity_update` method contains an inline soul-save block (lines 248–255) that still calls `moltis_config::save_soul(soul.as_deref())` — writing to the root path — while the response immediately below reads from `load_soul_for_agent(\"main\")` (agent-scoped path). Once `agents/main/SOUL.md` exists, any `identity_update` call that includes a `soul` field will write to the wrong location and return a stale value in its response. This is the same root-cause bug the PR aims to resolve, just at a different call site.\n\n<h3>Confidence Score: 2/5</h3>\n\n- Not safe to merge as-is — the inline soul-save in `identity_update` still writes to the root path, reproducing the exact bug this PR sets out to fix.\n- The `loader.rs` changes and most `service.rs` fixes are correct, but the `identity_update` method's inline soul block (line 254) still calls `save_soul()` instead of `save_soul_for_agent(\"main\", ...)`. This is a functional regression path: once any agent-level SOUL.md is created, soul updates via `identity_update` will silently write to the wrong file and the response will return stale data.\n- crates/onboarding/src/service.rs — specifically the soul-handling block inside `identity_update` at line 254.\n\n<h3>Important Files Changed</h3>\n\n\n\n\n| Filename | Overview |\n|----------|----------|\n| crates/onboarding/src/service.rs | Fixes `identity_update_soul` and both `save_identity` call sites to use agent-scoped paths, but misses the inline soul-saving block in `identity_update()` (line 254) which still calls `save_soul()` to the root — causing the same stale-read bug for soul updates sent through `identity_update`. |\n| crates/config/src/loader.rs | Adds `save_soul_for_agent` mirroring the existing `save_identity_for_agent` pattern; implementation is correct, and two new tests cover both the write path and the clear/empty behaviour. |\n| crates/config/src/lib.rs | Re-exports `save_soul_for_agent`; straightforward one-liner change, no issues. |\n\n</details>\n\n\n\n<h3>Sequence Diagram</h3>\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant Client\n    participant OnboardingService\n    participant ConfigLoader\n\n    Note over Client,ConfigLoader: identity_update_soul (FIXED)\n    Client->>OnboardingService: identity_update_soul(soul)\n    OnboardingService->>ConfigLoader: save_soul_for_agent(\"main\", soul)\n    ConfigLoader-->>OnboardingService: agents/main/SOUL.md written\n    OnboardingService-->>Client: ok\n\n    Note over Client,ConfigLoader: identity_update with soul param (STILL BROKEN)\n    Client->>OnboardingService: \"identity_update({soul: ...})\"\n    OnboardingService->>ConfigLoader: save_soul(soul)\n    ConfigLoader-->>OnboardingService: data_dir/SOUL.md written to root\n    OnboardingService->>ConfigLoader: load_soul_for_agent(\"main\")\n    ConfigLoader-->>OnboardingService: reads agents/main/SOUL.md - stale\n    OnboardingService-->>Client: soul field has stale value\n\n    Note over Client,ConfigLoader: wizard_next and identity save (FIXED)\n    Client->>OnboardingService: wizard_next or identity_update\n    OnboardingService->>ConfigLoader: save_identity_for_agent(\"main\", identity)\n    ConfigLoader-->>OnboardingService: agents/main/IDENTITY.md written\n    OnboardingService-->>Client: response\n```\n\n<!-- greptile_failed_comments -->\n<details open><summary><h3>Comments Outside Diff (1)</h3></summary>\n\n1. `crates/onboarding/src/service.rs`, line 248-255 ([link](https://github.com/moltis-org/moltis/blob/34533df65493145ab7c729ab86269b37a7a35f7f/crates/onboarding/src/service.rs#L248-L255)) \n\n   **`identity_update` still writes soul to the root path**\n\n   `identity_update()` handles a `soul` parameter inline at this block but still calls `moltis_config::save_soul(soul.as_deref())`, which writes to `data_dir/SOUL.md` (the root). > AGENT

Good — all findings confirmed. Let me fix them:

**1.** Line 254: `save_soul()` → `save_soul_for_agent("main", ...)`
**2.** `load_soul_for_agent("main")` uses `load_workspace_markdown` which returns `None` for empty files, then falls back to root — need to handle empty as explicit clear
**3.** Launchd plist: `node run` only accepts `--timeout` — must revert to using `node.json` config (the service reads from file)
**4.** Swift bridge: uses `load_soul()`/`save_soul()` — should use agent-aware versions
**5.** `instance_slug`: uses `load_identity()` — should use `load_identity_for_agent("main")`

Confirmed: empty file → `None` → falls back to root `load_soul()`. The fix: `load_soul_for_agent("main")` should check if the agent-level file *exists* (even if empty) and only fall back to root if it doesn't exist.

  "new_string": "pub fn load_soul_for_agent(agent_id: &str) -> Option<String> {\n    if agent_id == \"main\" {\n        let main_path = agent_workspace_dir(\"main\").join(\"SOUL.md\");\n        if main_path.exists() {\n            // File exists — return content or None (explicit clear).\n            return load_workspace_markdown(main_path);\n        }\n        return load_soul();\n    }\n    load_workspace_markdown(agent_workspace_dir(agent_id).join(\"SOUL.md\"))\n}"

  "new_string": "/// Generate a launchd plist XML string.\n///\n/// `node run` reads connection parameters from `~/.moltis/node.json` at\n/// runtime, so only `--timeout` is passed as a CLI flag here.\npub fn generate_launchd_plist(\n    moltis_bin: &Path,\n    config: &ServiceConfig,\n    log_path: &Path,\n) -> String {\n    let bin = moltis_bin.display();\n    let log = log_path.display();\n\n    let mut args = vec![\n        format!(\"    <string>{bin}</string>\"),\n        \"    <string>node</string>\".to_string(),\n        \"    <string>run</string>\".to_string(),\n        \"    <string>--timeout</string>\".to_string(),\n        format!(\"    <string>{}</string>\", config.timeout),\n    ];\n\n    // Environment variables for values that `node run` reads from\n    // `node.json` — included in the plist so launchd's env inherits them\n    // and they can be inspected via `launchctl print`.\n    let mut env_vars = vec![\n        (\"MOLTIS_NODE_GATEWAY_URL\", config.gateway_url.as_str()),\n        (\"MOLTIS_NODE_DEVICE_TOKEN\", config.device_token.as_str()),\n    ];\n    if let Some(ref id) = config.node_id {\n        env_vars.push((\"MOLTIS_NODE_ID\", id.as_str()));\n    }\n    if let Some(ref name) = config.display_name {\n        env_vars.push((\"MOLTIS_NODE_DISPLAY_NAME\", name.as_str()));\n    }\n    if let Some(ref dir) = config.working_dir {\n        args.push(\"    <string>--working-dir</string>\".to_string());\n        args.push(format!(\"    <string>{dir}</string>\"));\n    }\n\n    let args_str = args.join(\"\\n\");"
}

> AGENT

Pushed. Here's what was fixed from the PR review comments:

1. **Inline `save_soul()` in `identity_update`** (Greptile + Codex P1) — was still writing soul to root `SOUL.md` when the `soul` param was included in an identity update. Fixed to use `save_soul_for_agent("main", ...)`.

2. **Empty file fallback in `load_soul_for_agent`** (Codex P2) — clearing soul wrote an empty `agents/main/SOUL.md`, but `load_workspace_markdown` returned `None` for empty files, causing fallback to root. Now checks if the file *exists* first and only falls back when it doesn't.

3. **Launchd plist invalid CLI flags** (Codex P1) — `node run` only accepts `--timeout`, not `--gateway-url`/`--device-token`/etc. Reverted to passing only `--timeout`; other config is read from `node.json` at runtime.

4. **Swift bridge** (Codex P1) — `moltis_get_soul`/`moltis_save_soul` used root paths. Fixed to use `load/save_soul_for_agent("main")`.

5. **`instance_slug`** (Codex P2) — used `load_identity()` (root). Fixed to use `load_identity_for_agent("main")`.

     1→//! Terminal-based onboarding wizard using the shared state machine.
     2→
     3→use std::io::{BufRead, Write};
     4→
     5→use moltis_config::{MoltisConfig, find_or_default_config_path, save_config};
     6→
     7→use crate::{Context, Result, state::WizardState};
     8→
     9→/// Run the interactive onboarding wizard in the terminal.
    10→pub async fn run_onboarding() -> Result<()> {
    11→    let config_path = find_or_default_config_path();
    12→
    13→    // Check if already onboarded.
    14→    let mut identity_name: Option<String> = None;
    15→    let mut user_name: Option<String> = None;
    16→    if config_path.exists()
    17→        && let Ok(cfg) = moltis_config::loader::load_config(&config_path)
    18→    {
    19→        identity_name = cfg.identity.name;
    20→        user_name = cfg.user.name;
    21→    }
    22→    if let Some(id) = moltis_config::load_identity()
    23→        && id.name.is_some()
    24→    {
    25→        identity_name = id.name;
    26→    }
    27→    if let Some(user) = moltis_config::load_user()
    28→        && user.name.is_some()
    29→    {
    30→        user_name = user.name;
    31→    }
    32→
    33→    if identity_name.is_some() && user_name.is_some() {
    34→        println!(
    35→            "Already onboarded as {} with agent {}.",
    36→            user_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("?"),
    37→            identity_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("?"),
    38→        );
    39→        return Ok(());
    40→    }
    41→
    42→    let mut state = WizardState::new();
    43→    let stdin = std::io::stdin();
    44→    let mut reader = stdin.lock();
    45→
    46→    while !state.is_done() {
    47→        println!("{}", state.prompt());
    48→        print!("> ");
    49→        std::io::stdout().flush()?;
    50→        let mut line = String::new();
    51→        reader.read_line(&mut line)?;
    52→        state.advance(&line);
    53→    }
    54→
    55→    // Merge into existing config or create new one.
    56→    let mut config = if config_path.exists() {
    57→        moltis_config::loader::load_config(&config_path).unwrap_or_default()
    58→    } else {
    59→        MoltisConfig::default()
    60→    };
    61→    config.identity = state.identity;
    62→    config.user = state.user;
    63→
    64→    let path = save_config(&config).context("failed to save onboarding config")?;
    65→    moltis_config::save_identity(&config.identity).context("failed to save identity")?;
    66→    moltis_config::save_user(&config.user).context("failed to save user")?;
    67→    println!("Config saved to {}", path.display());
    68→    println!("Onboarding complete!");
    69→    Ok(())
    70→}
    71→

     1→use {anyhow::Result, clap::Subcommand};
     2→
     3→use moltis_tools::sandbox;
     4→
     5→fn sanitize_instance_slug(name: &str) -> String {
     6→    let base = name.to_lowercase();
     7→    let mut out = String::new();
     8→    let mut last_dash = false;
     9→    for ch in base.chars() {
    10→        let mapped = if ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() {
    11→            ch
    12→        } else {
    13→            '-'
    14→        };
    15→        if mapped == '-' {
    16→            if !last_dash {
    17→                out.push(mapped);
    18→            }
    19→            last_dash = true;
    20→        } else {
    21→            out.push(mapped);
    22→            last_dash = false;
    23→        }
    24→    }
    25→    let out = out.trim_matches('-').to_string();
    26→    if out.is_empty() {
    27→        "moltis".to_string()
    28→    } else {
    29→        out
    30→    }
    31→}
    32→
    33→fn instance_sandbox_prefix(config: &moltis_config::MoltisConfig) -> String {
    34→    let mut identity_name = config.identity.name.clone();
    35→    if let Some(file_identity) = moltis_config::load_identity()
    36→        && file_identity.name.is_some()
    37→    {
    38→        identity_name = file_identity.name;
    39→    }
    40→    let slug = sanitize_instance_slug(identity_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("moltis"));
    41→    format!("moltis-{slug}-sandbox")
    42→}

   184→
   185→fn write_soul_for_agent(agent_id: &str, soul: Option<String>) -> Result<(), ErrorShape> {
   186→    if agent_id == "main" {
   187→        moltis_config::save_soul(soul.as_deref())
   188→            .map_err(|e| ErrorShape::new(error_codes::UNAVAILABLE, e.to_string()))?;
   189→        return Ok(());
   190→    }
   191→    let dir = moltis_config::agent_workspace_dir(agent_id);
   192→    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir)
   193→        .map_err(|e| ErrorShape::new(error_codes::UNAVAILABLE, e.to_string()))?;
   194→    let soul_path = dir.join("SOUL.md");
   195→    match soul.as_deref().map(str::trim) {
   196→        Some(content) if !content.is_empty() => {
   197→            std::fs::write(&soul_path, content)
   198→                .map_err(|e| ErrorShape::new(error_codes::UNAVAILABLE, e.to_string()))?;
   199→        },
   200→        _ => {
   201→            std::fs::write(&soul_path, "")
   202→                .map_err(|e| ErrorShape::new(error_codes::UNAVAILABLE, e.to_string()))?;
   203→        },
   204→    }
   205→    Ok(())
   206→}
   207→
   208→fn normalize_relative_agent_path(path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, ErrorShape> {
   209→    let trimmed = path.trim();
   210→    if trimmed.is_empty() {
   211→        return Err(ErrorShape::new(
   212→            error_codes::INVALID_REQUEST,
   213→            "missing 'path' parameter",
   214→        ));
   215→    }
   216→    let candidate = Path::new(trimmed);

**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Preserve explicit main identity clears across legacy fallback**

Switching `identity_update` to `save_identity_for_agent("main", ...)` means a full clear (all of `name`/`emoji`/`theme` unset) now removes `agents/main/IDENTITY.md`; on upgraded installs that still have a legacy root `IDENTITY.md`, subsequent reads go through `load_identity_for_agent("main")` and fall back to that stale root file, so cleared values reappear instead of staying cleared. This makes "clear identity" non-persistent for migrated users unless the legacy root file is also handled or a main-agent clear sentinel is kept.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.
```
